# Agents · E · part 14

1,025 entries, explorer to extract. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **explorer**: Reads files, logs, or test output that fall outside the current phase's Read: line and returns at most 10 lines. Use whenever the guard blocks a read, a gate failure needs diagnosis, or something must be located in the repo. Keeps dirty context out of the main session. - **explorer**: Read-only codebase investigator. Traces behavior and dependencies and returns concise evidence with file:line references. Never edits, never runs commands. Returns the schema fully populated plus an explicit list of items it could not classify. Sustains high-volume mechanical passes. Instances — catalog every occurrence of a construct, extract one field from each of many files.
